### `SmartKitProvider` — component

`import { SmartKitProvider } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/context';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `services` | **yes** | `Record&lt;string, ServiceConfig>` | Services by name: `{ model, dataProvider, variants? }` each, usually from `loadService({ serviceUrl, fetch })` of `@rhfan2019/smart-kit-core`. Required. |
| `locale` | **yes** | `Locale` | UI language of the library texts and number / date formats: "en" or "zh". Required. |
| `namespace` | **yes** | `string` | Prefix of every page state key and query key, e.g. "my-app". |
| `defaultService` |  | `string` | Service used when a building block does not name one. Defaults to the first service. |
| `messages` |  | `Partial&lt;Messages>` | Overrides of library texts. |
| `timeZone` |  | `string` | IANA time zone for Edm.DateTimeOffset display and filtering. Defaults to the browser's zone. |
| `navigation` |  | `NavigationAdapter` | Connects the library to the host router: `toObject`, `toList`, `href`, `hasObjectPage`, `toTable`. Without it rows do not navigate. |
| `pageState` |  | `PageStateStore` | Where page state (filters, sort, scroll) is kept for back navigation; default in memory. `createSessionPageStateStore()` keeps it in sessionStorage. |
| `renderers` |  | `RendererRegistry` | Custom renderers for fields by type name or property (white-box extension point). |
| `queryClient` |  | `QueryClient` | TanStack Query client; default: one created by the provider. |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | The application (pages using smart-kit building blocks). |

### `SmartKitProviderProps` — interface

Props of `SmartKitProvider` (see above).

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/i18n`

`createText` (function) · `INTL_LOCALE` (const) · `Locale` (type) · `MessageKey` (type) · `Messages` (type) · `MESSAGES` (const) · `TextFn` (type) · `TextParams` (type) · `TypedKey` (type) · `typedText` (function)

### `createText` — function

```ts

```

### `INTL_LOCALE` — const

```ts

```

### `Locale` — type

```ts
export type Locale = 'zh' | 'en';
```

### `MessageKey` — type

```ts
export type MessageKey = keyof Messages;
```

### `Messages` — type

The library texts; the control texts (docs/26) are loaded per control group, but can be overridden here too.

```ts
export type Messages = Widen<typeof en & typeof controlsEn>;
```

### `MESSAGES` — const

The library texts per locale; the control texts are not included (they come with each control group, docs/26).

```ts
const MESSAGES: Record<Locale, Widen<{ readonly 'filterBar.search': "Search"; readonly 'filterBar.go': "Go"; readonly 'filterBar.adaptFilters': { readonl…
```

### `TextFn` — type

```ts
export type TextFn = (key: MessageKey, params?: TextParams) => string;
```

### `TextParams` — type

```ts
export type TextParams = Record<string, string | number>;
```

### `TypedKey` — type

Keys that have a variant `<key>Type` naming the kind of record (UI.HeaderInfo/TypeName).

```ts
export type TypedKey = 'create.done' | 'delete.done' | 'edit.conflict' | 'object.notFound' | 'object.loadError';
```

### `typedText` — function

The message naming the record type ("用户已创建。"); without a type name the neutral text ("已创建。").

```ts
typedText(t: TextFn, key: TypedKey, typeName: string | undefined, params?: TextParams): string
```

### `conditionText` — function

Token text of a condition, e.g. "Office (C1)", ">10", "!(=X)", "This month".

```ts
conditionText(c: Condition, prop: PropertyModel | undefined, locale: string, timeZone?: string, options?: ConditionTextOptions): string
```

Options of `conditionText`.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `t` |  | `TextFn` | Texts for relative dates ("This month", "Last 7 days"); without it the operator name is shown ("THISMONTH"). |

### `ExtendEntry` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `position` | **yes** | `ExtendPosition` |  |
| `item` | **yes** | `T \| ((original: T) => T)` | New item (insert) or replacement (replace). For replace, a function receives the original. |

### `ExtendPosition` — type

Id-based replace / insert for model-generated items. See docs/06-extension-and-theming.md §1.

```ts

```

### `FclArrow` — interface

One arrow of a column separator: the layout it switches to and its accessible label.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `layout` | **yes** | `LayoutType` |  |
| `direction` | **yes** | `'left' \| 'right'` |  |
| `label` | **yes** | `'layout.expandBegin' \| 'layout.expandMid' \| 'layout.expandEnd' \| 'layout.showEnd'` |  |

### `fclArrows` — function

Arrows of the separators after the begin column ([0]) and after the mid column ([1]) in a layout (UI5 FCL). The second separator's arrows need an end column (`hasEnd`).

```ts
fclArrows(layout: LayoutType, hasEnd: boolean): [FclArrow | undefined, FclArrow | undefined]
```

### `filterSummaryText` — function

```ts
filterSummaryText(t: ReturnType<typeof useText>, labels: string[]): string
```

### `getPath` — function

Reads a value by property path, following expanded navigation objects ("_Partner/PartnerName").

```ts
getPath(row: Row | undefined, path: string): unknown
```

### `hiddenByImportance` — function

Widths below which Low, then Medium columns move out of the table (sap.m.Table pop-in by importance).

```ts
hiddenByImportance(width: number, importance: Priority | undefined): boolean
```

### `isActionAvailable` — function

Whether an action can be applied to a row (Core.OperationAvailable; missing means available).

```ts
isActionAvailable(action: ActionModel, row: Row | undefined): boolean
```

### `isEditable` — function

Whether a property can be changed: not computed, not a key, not read-only (immutable only when creating).

```ts
isEditable(prop: PropertyModel | undefined, creating: boolean): boolean
```

### `linkify` — function

Plain text with http(s) URLs as links (no HTML from the server is rendered).

```ts
linkify(text: string): ReactNode[]
```

### `messageTypeOf` — function

core `Message.type` (lower case, ADR-023) → MessageType.

```ts
messageTypeOf(type: Message["type"]): MessageType
```

### `MICRO_COMPARISON_MAX` — const

Maximum number of bars of a comparison chart (MC-41).

```ts
const MICRO_COMPARISON_MAX: 12
```

### `niceTicks` — function

4–6 "nice" ticks covering [min, max] (both extended to include 0).

```ts
niceTicks(min: number, max: number, count?: number): number[]
```

### `parseConditionText` — function

Parses user input into a condition. Plain text means "equal to".

```ts
parseConditionText(input: string, prop: PropertyModel | undefined): Condition | undefined
```

### `PreventableEvent` — type

An event object for handlers whose UI5 event can be prevented (`oEvent.preventDefault()`).

```ts
export type PreventableEvent<T> = T & { preventDefault(): void };
```

### `relativeDateText` — function

Token text of a relative date condition, e.g. "This month", "Last 7 days", "Last 2 weeks", "From Jan 1, 2026", "From Today" (the texts of DynamicDateRange, sap.m.StandardDynamicDateRangeKeys).

```ts
relativeDateText(c: Condition, t?: TextFn, intlLocale?: string): string
```

### `RendererRegistry` — type

Keys, from most to least specific: - `path:<EntityType full name>/<path>` — one field - `rating`, `progress`, `image`, `link`, `currency`, `unit`, `criticality`, `boolean` - `type:<Edm type>`, e.g. `type:Edm.Date`

```ts
export type RendererRegistry = Record<string, FieldRenderer>;
```

### `resolveRef` — function

```ts
resolveRef(row: Row | undefined, ref: ValueRef | undefined): unknown
```

### `RESTRICTED_FILE_NAME_CHARACTERS` — const

Characters not allowed in file names by default (UI5).

```ts
const RESTRICTED_FILE_NAME_CHARACTERS: "\\:/*?\"<>|[]{}@#$"
```

### `toDates` — function

The dates of a value (sap.m.DynamicDateRange.toDates): start of the first day and end of the last day in local time; FROM / TO give one date, as in UI5. `now` is an internal hook for tests.

```ts
toDates(value: DynamicDateRangeValue, calendarWeekNumbering?: CalendarWeekNumbering, now?: Date): Date[]
```

### `usePageState` — hook

```ts
usePageState<T>(key: string | undefined, initial: T | (() => T)): [T, (next: T | ((prev: T) => T)) => void]
```

### `validate` — function

Client validation (OP In Edit Mode): mandatory fields and number formats; messages target the property path.

```ts
validate(model: MetaModel, typeName: string, fields: FieldModel[], values: Row, t: TextFn, creating?: boolean): Message[]
```

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/f/Card`

`$CardSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `HeaderPosition` (type) · `SemanticRole` (type)

### `$CardSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `header` |  | `ReactNode` | Header (`Header` or `NumericHeader`). |
| `headerPosition` |  | `HeaderPosition` | Position of the header. |
| `content` |  | `ReactNode` | Content (`ListContent`, `TableContent`, …, or any React content: the Component card); the same as `children`. |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | The content as JSX children (used when `content` is not given). |
| `footer` |  | `ReactNode` | Footer, e.g. buttons (the integration card's actions strip). |
| `width` |  | `string` | CSS width (the width of the container). |
| `height` |  | `string` | CSS height (the height of the content). |
| `semanticRole` |  | `SemanticRole` | Accessibility role: a region, or an item of a list (then `onPress` makes the whole card interactive). |
| `headingLevel` |  | `TitleLevel` | Heading level of the header title (aria-level). |
| `onPress` |  | `() => void` | Press of the whole card; only with `semanticRole` "ListItem" (sap.f.CardBase#press). |
| `className` |  | `string` | Extra CSS class on the root element. |
| `style` |  | `CSSProperties` | Inline style, e.g. `{ width: 400 }`; the `width` / `height` props win over the style's. |
| `aria-roledescription` |  | `string` | Accessible description of the card type, e.g. "List card". |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;Card>` |  |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/f/DynamicPage`

`$DynamicPageSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `DynamicPage$BreakpointChangeEvent` (interface) · `DynamicPage$PinnedStateChangeEvent` (interface)

### `$DynamicPageSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` |  | `ReactNode` | The title, a DynamicPageTitle (or ObjectPageDynamicHeaderTitle). |
| `header` |  | `ReactNode` | The header, a DynamicPageHeader. |
| `content` |  | `ReactNode` | Page content. |
| `footer` |  | `ReactNode` | Footer toolbar content (e.g. Save / Cancel while editing); shown with `showFooter`. |
| `showFooter` |  | `boolean` | Shows the footer. |
| `preserveHeaderStateOnScroll` |  | `boolean` | The header keeps its state while scrolling (no snap on scroll, no pin). |
| `headerExpanded` |  | `boolean` | The header is expanded; without it the page keeps the state itself. |
| `headerPinned` |  | `boolean` | The header is pinned; without it the page keeps the state itself. |
| `toggleHeaderOnTitleClick` |  | `boolean` | Clicking the title bar expands or collapses the header. |
| `backgroundDesign` |  | `PageBackgroundDesign` | Page background. |
| `fitContent` |  | `boolean` | The content fills the remaining height and scrolls itself (grid tables); otherwise the page scrolls. |
| `stickySubheaderProvider` |  | `ReactNode` | Sticky content under the header (an anchor bar or tab bar). |
| `landmarkInfo` |  | `ReactNode` | ARIA landmark roles and labels of the page's containers, a DynamicPageAccessibleLandmarkInfo; without it no landmarks are written. |
| `onPinnedStateChange` |  | `(e: DynamicPage$PinnedStateChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the pin button is pressed. |
| `onBreakpointChange` |  | `(e: DynamicPage$BreakpointChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the page width moves into another media range. |
| `titleMessage` |  | `ReactNode` | Extension: shown between title and header content; stays visible when snapped (object page message strip). |
| `letterboxing` |  | `boolean` | Extension: limits the page content to 1280px (DP Responsiveness · letterboxing). |
| `filterToggle` |  | `boolean` | Extension: "Show Filters / Hide Filters" in the title actions instead of the expand/collapse and pin buttons. |
| `className` |  | `string` | Extra CSS class on the root element. |
| `data-testid` |  | `string` |  |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/f/DynamicPageTitle`

`$DynamicPageTitleSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `DynamicPageTitle$StateChangeEvent` (interface)

### `$DynamicPageTitleSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `heading` |  | `ReactNode` | The title (an h1 for a string; any element, e.g. a variant management, otherwise). |
| `snappedHeading` |  | `ReactNode` | Replaces `heading` while the header is snapped (e.g. a small avatar and the title). |
| `expandedHeading` |  | `ReactNode` | Replaces `heading` while the header is expanded. |
| `actions` |  | `ReactNode` | Actions on the right; they move into an overflow from right to left when space runs out (an OverflowToolbar). |
| `navigationActions` |  | `ReactNode` | Layout actions at the very right (DP Header Title (6)), e.g. FlexibleColumnLayoutActions. |
| `content` |  | `ReactNode` | Content in the middle of the title bar, e.g. key information (DP Header Title (4)). |
| `snappedContent` |  | `ReactNode` | Shown under the heading while the header is snapped (e.g. the filter information). |
| `expandedContent` |  | `ReactNode` | Shown under the heading while the header is expanded (e.g. the description). |
| `snappedTitleOnMobile` |  | `ReactNode` | On phones, a snapped title shows only this title and a toggle (DP Summary Line). |
| `breadcrumbs` |  | `ReactNode` | Breadcrumb links above the heading; inside a FlexibleColumnLayout only in full screen with one column. |
| `areaShrinkRatio` |  | `DynamicPageTitleShrinkRatio` | Flex-shrink factors of the heading, content and actions areas. |
| `backgroundDesign` |  | `BackgroundDesign` | Background of the title. |
| `onStateChange` |  | `(e: DynamicPageTitle$StateChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the header is expanded or collapsed (by the user or by scrolling). |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/f/FlexibleColumnLayout`

`$FlexibleColumnLayoutSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `FlexibleColumnLayout$ColumnResizeEvent` (interface) · `FlexibleColumnLayout$ColumnsDistributionChangeEvent` (interface) · `FlexibleColumnLayout$StateChangeEvent` (interface)

### `$FlexibleColumnLayoutSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `layout` |  | `LayoutType` | The column layout. |
| `beginColumnPages` |  | `ReactNode` | Pages of the begin column (the first one is shown). |
| `midColumnPages` |  | `ReactNode` | Pages of the mid column. |
| `endColumnPages` |  | `ReactNode` | Pages of the end column. |
| `backgroundDesign` |  | `BackgroundDesign` | Background of the layout. |
| `autoFocus` |  | `boolean` | The focus moves to the first focusable element of a column's page when the column shows a page for the first time, and when it shows another page (another React key or element type) while the focus was in the column. |
| `landmarkInfo` |  | `ReactNode` | Labels of the column regions (and the separator arrows), a FlexibleColumnLayoutAccessibleLandmarkInfo. |
| `onStateChange` |  | `(e: FlexibleColumnLayout$StateChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the user asks for another layout (dragging a separator, layout actions, arrows) or the maximum column count changes. |
| `onColumnResize` |  | `(e: FlexibleColumnLayout$ColumnResizeEvent) => void` | Fired when column widths change; true for each column that changed. |
| `onColumnsDistributionChange` |  | `(e: FlexibleColumnLayout$ColumnsDistributionChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the user resized the columns by moving a separator. |
| `midPaging` |  | `ColumnPaging` | Extension: paging of the mid column for its Paginator (the host's list). |
| `endPaging` |  | `ColumnPaging` | Extension: paging of the end column for its Paginator. |
| `separatorArrows` |  | `boolean` | Extension: arrow buttons on the separators that switch to the neighbouring layout (default false). |
| `className` |  | `string` | Extra CSS class on the root element. |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;FlexibleColumnLayout>` |  |

| `isResize` | **yes** | `boolean` | The change comes from a resize (the maximum column count changed). |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/f/cards/Header`

`$HeaderSettings` (interface) · `AvatarColor` (type) · `AvatarImageFitType` (type) · `AvatarShape` (type) · `AvatarSize` (type) · `default` (component)

### `$HeaderSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` |  | `string` | Title; default: from the content's annotations (UI.HeaderInfo). |
| `subtitle` |  | `string` | Subtitle text. |
| `iconState` |  | `ValueState` | A status-coloured message icon in the icon area instead of the avatar (do not combine with iconSrc / iconInitials). |
| `statusText` |  | `string` | Counter text, e.g. "5 of 40"; default: from the content (CD-03). |
| `titleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the title is truncated (CD-02). |
| `subtitleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the subtitle is truncated (CD-02). |
| `dataTimestamp` |  | `string` | Time of the oldest data in the card (ISO 8601), shown as a relative time such as "5 minutes ago". |
| `wrappingType` |  | `WrappingType` | Wrapping of the title, subtitle and details texts. |
| `infoSection` |  | `ReactNode[]` | Info sections shown at the bottom of the header (`infoSection` aggregation). |
| `toolbar` |  | `ReactNode` | Additional content in the header's toolbar area, e.g. a menu button (`toolbar` aggregation); pressing it does not press the header. |
| `onPress` |  | `() => void` | Press of the header (event `press`). |
| `iconSrc` |  | `string \| ReactElement` | Image URL of the avatar, or an icon element (for example from lucide-react; UI5 takes an icon URI, ADR-032 rule 5). |
| `iconInitials` |  | `string` | Initials shown in the avatar. |
| `iconDisplayShape` |  | `AvatarShape` | Avatar shape. |
| `iconAlt` |  | `string` | Alternative text of the avatar (its accessible name). |
| `iconBackgroundColor` |  | `AvatarColor` | Background colour of the avatar. |
| `iconSize` |  | `AvatarSize` | Size of the avatar ('Custom' is shown as 'S'). |
| `iconFitType` |  | `AvatarImageFitType` | How an image fills the avatar. |

### `AvatarColor` — type

sap.m.AvatarColor

```ts
export type AvatarColor = | 'Accent1' | 'Accent2' | 'Accent3' | 'Accent4' | 'Accent5' | 'Accent6' | 'Accent7' | 'Accent8' | 'Accent9' | 'Accent10' | 'Placeholder' | 'Random' | 'TileIcon' | 'Transparent';
```

### `AvatarImageFitType` — type

sap.m.AvatarImageFitType

```ts
export type AvatarImageFitType = 'Cover' | 'Contain';
```

### `AvatarShape` — type

sap.m.AvatarShape

```ts
export type AvatarShape = 'Circle' | 'Square';
```

### `AvatarSize` — type

sap.m.AvatarSize

```ts
export type AvatarSize = 'XS' | 'S' | 'M' | 'L' | 'XL' | 'Custom';
```

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/f/cards/NumericHeader`

`$NumericHeaderSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `DeviationIndicator` (type) · `NumericHeaderSideIndicatorsAlignment` (type) · `ValueColor` (type)

### `$NumericHeaderSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` |  | `string` | Title; default: from the content's annotations (UI.HeaderInfo). |
| `subtitle` |  | `string` | Subtitle text. |
| `unitOfMeasurement` |  | `string` | Unit of the number, shown in the subtitle row: "&lt;subtitle> \| &lt;unit>" (CD-04). |
| `number` |  | `string` | The main indicator (KPI) value, already formatted. |
| `scale` |  | `string` | Scaling factor shown next to the number, e.g. "K". |
| `trend` |  | `DeviationIndicator` | Trend arrow. |
| `state` |  | `ValueColor` | Semantic colour of the number. |
| `numberSize` |  | `'S' \| 'L'` | Size of the number: "S" or "L". |
| `numberVisible` |  | `boolean` | Shows the main indicator (number, scale, trend). |
| `details` |  | `string` | Text below the number, e.g. the period of the KPI. |
| `detailsMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the details text is truncated. |
| `detailsState` |  | `ValueColor` | Semantic colour of the details text (UI5: restricted to SAP Work Zone, sap.ui.core.ValueState). |
| `sideIndicators` |  | `$NumericSideIndicatorSettings[]` | Up to two side indicators (CD-04). |
| `sideIndicatorsAlignment` |  | `NumericHeaderSideIndicatorsAlignment` | Alignment of the side indicators: after the number ('Begin') or at the end of the header ('End'). |
| `microChart` |  | `ReactNode` | A micro chart shown next to the indicators (`microChart` aggregation). |
| `entitySet` |  | `string` | Annotation-driven KPI (OVP KPI header, docs/20): the entity set and the UI.DataPoint whose value is summed on the server. Number, scale, unit, state, trend and the target / deviation side indicators come from the data point unless given as props. |
| `service` |  | `string` | Name of the service in `SmartKitProvider` `services`; default: the provider's `defaultService`. |
| `dataPointAnnotationPath` |  | `string` | e.g. "com.sap.vocabularies.UI.v1.DataPoint#Spend" or "@UI.DataPoint#Spend". |
| `selectionAnnotationPath` |  | `string` | UI.SelectionVariant that filters the KPI. |
| `filters` |  | `FilterState` | Additional filters (e.g. from a filter bar). |
| `statusText` |  | `string` | Counter text, e.g. "5 of 40"; default: from the content (CD-03). |
| `titleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the title is truncated (CD-02). |
| `subtitleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the subtitle is truncated (CD-02). |
| `dataTimestamp` |  | `string` | Time of the oldest data in the card (ISO 8601), shown as a relative time such as "5 minutes ago". |
| `wrappingType` |  | `WrappingType` | Wrapping of the title, subtitle and details texts. |
| `infoSection` |  | `ReactNode[]` | Info sections shown at the bottom of the header (`infoSection` aggregation). |
| `toolbar` |  | `ReactNode` | Additional content in the header's toolbar area, e.g. a menu button (`toolbar` aggregation); pressing it does not press the header. |
| `onPress` |  | `() => void` | Press of the header (event `press`). |
| `iconSrc` |  | `string \| ReactElement` | Image URL of the avatar, or an icon element (for example from lucide-react; UI5 takes an icon URI, ADR-032 rule 5). |
| `iconInitials` |  | `string` | Initials shown in the avatar. |
| `iconDisplayShape` |  | `AvatarShape` | Avatar shape. |
| `iconAlt` |  | `string` | Alternative text of the avatar (its accessible name). |
| `iconBackgroundColor` |  | `AvatarColor` | Background colour of the avatar. |
| `iconSize` |  | `AvatarSize` | Size of the avatar ('Custom' is shown as 'S'). |
| `iconFitType` |  | `AvatarImageFitType` | How an image fills the avatar. |

| `filters` |  | `FilterState` | Additional filters (e.g. from a filter bar). |
| `statusText` |  | `string` | Counter text, e.g. "5 of 40"; default: from the content (CD-03). |
| `titleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the title is truncated (CD-02). |
| `subtitleMaxLines` |  | `number` | Lines before the subtitle is truncated (CD-02). |
| `dataTimestamp` |  | `string` | Time of the oldest data in the card (ISO 8601), shown as a relative time such as "5 minutes ago". |
| `wrappingType` |  | `WrappingType` | Wrapping of the title, subtitle and details texts. |
| `infoSection` |  | `ReactNode[]` | Info sections shown at the bottom of the header (`infoSection` aggregation). |
| `toolbar` |  | `ReactNode` | Additional content in the header's toolbar area, e.g. a menu button (`toolbar` aggregation); pressing it does not press the header. |
| `onPress` |  | `() => void` | Press of the header (event `press`). |
| `iconSrc` |  | `string \| ReactElement` | Image URL of the avatar, or an icon element (for example from lucide-react; UI5 takes an icon URI, ADR-032 rule 5). |
| `iconInitials` |  | `string` | Initials shown in the avatar. |
| `iconDisplayShape` |  | `AvatarShape` | Avatar shape. |
| `iconAlt` |  | `string` | Alternative text of the avatar (its accessible name). |
| `iconBackgroundColor` |  | `AvatarColor` | Background colour of the avatar. |
| `iconSize` |  | `AvatarSize` | Size of the avatar ('Custom' is shown as 'S'). |
| `iconFitType` |  | `AvatarImageFitType` | How an image fills the avatar. |

### `BusyCustomIconProps` — interface

The custom icon properties shared by BusyIndicator and BusyDialog.

Props of `BusyCustomIcon` (see above).

`$DynamicDateRangeSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `DynamicDateFormatter` (interface) · `DynamicDateOption` (interface) · `DynamicDateRange$ChangeEvent` (interface) · `DynamicDateRangeValue` (interface)

### `$DynamicDateRangeSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` |  | `DynamicDateRangeValue` | The value (controlled); undefined: empty. |
| `width` |  | `string` | CSS width of the field. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `valueState` |  | `ValueState` |  |
| `name` |  | `string` | Name of the input (forms). |
| `placeholder` |  | `string` |  |
| `editable` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `valueStateText` |  | `string` |  |
| `required` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `enableGroupHeaders` |  | `boolean` | Shows the group headers (single dates, date ranges, weeks …) in the option list. |
| `formatter` |  | `DynamicDateFormatter` | Formats and parses the dates in the field (UI5: a DateFormat instance). |
| `standardOptions` |  | `readonly StandardDynamicDateRangeKeys[]` | The standard options offered; the list keeps the UI5 grouping and this order inside each group. Default: all 68 keys (`DEFAULT_STANDARD_OPTIONS`). |
| `hideInput` |  | `boolean` | Renders no field; the popover is opened with `open(domRef)` / `openBy(domRef)`. |
| `calendarWeekNumbering` |  | `CalendarWeekNumbering` | Week numbering for week options (default: the locale). |
| `showClearIcon` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `customOptions` |  | `DynamicDateOption[]` | Own options (sap.m.DynamicDateOption). |
| `onChange` |  | `(e: DynamicDateRange$ChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the user picks, types or clears a value; `valid` is false when typed text could not be read. |
| `label` |  | `ReactNode` | Extension: field label (Mantine input label). |
| `id` |  | `string` |  |
| `aria-label` |  | `string` |  |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;DynamicDateRange>` |  |

### `DynamicDateFormatter` — interface

The date formatter of the `formatter` property (UI5: a sap.ui.core.format.DateFormat instance).

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `format` | **yes** | `(date: Date) => string` |  |
| `parse` |  | `(text: string) => Date \| null` |  |

### `DynamicDateOption` — interface

A custom option (sap.m.DynamicDateOption); methods are optional except where the option needs them.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `key` | **yes** | `string` |  |
| `valueTypes` |  | `string[]` | The value types, e.g. `['int']`, `['date']`; empty: the option is picked without a detail page. |
| `getText` |  | `() => string` | The option text in the list (UI5 `getText`). |
| `getGroup` |  | `() => string \| number` | The group of the option (UI5 `getGroup`): a standard group name (SingleDates, DateRanges, Weeks, Month, Quarters, Years), a group added with the control's `addGroup`, or a group number (1–6 the standard groups, 7… the added ones). |
| `getGroupHeader` |  | `() => string` | The group header shown above the option (UI5 `getGroupHeader`); default "Other Options" (a group added with `addGroup` uses its header). |
| `format` |  | `(value: DynamicDateRangeValue) => string` |  |
| `parse` |  | `(text: string) => DynamicDateRangeValue \| null \| undefined` |  |
| `toDates` |  | `(value: DynamicDateRangeValue, calendarWeekNumbering?: CalendarWeekNumbering) => Date[]` |  |
| `createValueHelpUI` |  | `(ctx: { value: DynamicDateRangeValue \| undefined; update(value: DynamicDateRangeValue \| undefined): void }) => ReactNode` | The detail page content. `update` takes the value the page stands for (undefined while it is incomplete); React's answer to UI5's `createValueHelpUI(oControl, fnControlsUpdated)`. |
| `validateValueHelpUI` |  | `(value: DynamicDateRangeValue \| undefined) => boolean` | Whether the detail page is complete (default: a value was given to `update`). |
| `enhanceFormattedValue` |  | `() => boolean` | Whether the field also shows the resolved dates, e.g. "Today (Nov 17, 2026)". |

### `DynamicDateRange$ChangeEvent` — interface

Parameters of the `change` event.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | **yes** | `DynamicDateRangeValue \| undefined` | The current value; undefined when the field was cleared or the text could not be read. |
| `valid` | **yes** | `boolean` | Whether the new value is valid. |

### `DynamicDateRangeValue` — interface

The value of a DynamicDateRange, as in UI5: `{ operator: 'LASTDAYS', values: [7] }`, `{ operator: 'DATE', values: [Date] }`, `{ operator: 'DATERANGE', values: [Date, Date] }`, `{ operator: 'SPECIFICMONTH', values: [monthIndex] }`, `{ operator: 'SPECIFICMONTHINYEAR', values: [monthIndex, year] }`, `{ operator: 'TODAYFROMTO', values: [daysBefore, daysAfter] }`. Custom options use their own key and values.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `operator` | **yes** | `StandardDynamicDateRangeKeys \| (string & {})` |  |
| `values` | **yes** | `Array&lt;Date \| number \| string>` |  |

### `default` — component

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/IllustratedMessage';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `description` |  | `string` | Description; defaults to the type's text when enableDefaultTitleAndDescription is on. |
| `enableDefaultTitleAndDescription` |  | `boolean` | Uses the default title and description of the type for the missing ones. |
| `enableFormattedText` |  | `boolean` | Accepted for compatibility: the description stays plain text, HTML is not parsed. |
| `enableVerticalResponsiveness` |  | `boolean` | Auto size also follows the container height; the container then needs a height. |
| `illustrationSize` |  | `IllustratedMessageSize` | Size (Auto: chosen by the container width — below 160px Base without illustration, then ExtraSmall, Small, Medium, and from 680px Large). |
| `illustrationType` |  | `IllustratedMessageType` | Illustration; `src` takes precedence. |
| `src` |  | `string` | Illustration as a URI, taking precedence over `illustrationType`: `sap-illustration://&lt;type>` (or `sap-illustration://sapIllus/&lt;type>`) for a type of the default set. Other sets are not available; the control then falls back to `illustrationType`. |
| `title` |  | `string` | Title; defaults to the type's text when enableDefaultTitleAndDescription is on. |
| `ariaTitleLevel` |  | `'Auto' \| 'H1' \| 'H2' \| 'H3' \| 'H4' \| 'H5' \| 'H6'` | sap.ui.core.TitleLevel of the title (Auto renders a level-2 heading). |
| `decorative` |  | `boolean` | The illustration is decorative: hidden from screen readers (false: an image labelled with the title). |
| `additionalContent` |  | `ReactNode` | Actions below the description, usually buttons. |
| `illustrationAriaLabelledBy` |  | `string \| string[]` | IDs of the elements that label the illustration (aria-labelledby on the illustration; ignored when decorative). |
| `illustrationAriaDescribedBy` |  | `string \| string[]` | IDs of the elements that describe the illustration (aria-describedby on the illustration; ignored when decorative). |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode \| undefined` |  |
| `defaultChecked` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |

`default` (const)


## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessageBox`

`default` (const) · `MessageBoxAction` (type) · `MessageBoxIcon` (type) · `MessageBoxOptions` (interface)

### `default` — const

sap.m.MessageBox. Every method returns a promise of the pressed action (extension: UI5 returns nothing and only calls `onClose`).

```ts
const default: { Action: { readonly OK: "OK"; readonly CANCEL: "CANCEL"; readonly YES: "YES"; readonly NO: "NO"; readonly ABORT: "ABORT"; readonly RETRY…
```

### `MessageBoxAction` — type

sap.m.MessageBox.Action; a custom action is any other string (shown as the button text).

```ts
export type MessageBoxAction = 'OK' | 'CANCEL' | 'YES' | 'NO' | 'ABORT' | 'RETRY' | 'IGNORE' | 'CLOSE' | 'DELETE';
```

### `MessageBoxIcon` — type

sap.m.MessageBox.Icon

```ts
export type MessageBoxIcon = 'NONE' | 'INFORMATION' | 'WARNING' | 'ERROR' | 'SUCCESS' | 'QUESTION';
```

### `MessageBoxOptions` — interface

Options of `MessageBox.show` and the shortcuts (sap.m.MessageBox mOptions, 1.154).

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` |  | `MessageBoxIcon` | Only `show`: the icon (default NONE; INFORMATION when `details` is set). |
| `title` |  | `string` | Dialog title; the shortcuts default to a title per type ("Alert", "Confirmation", "Error" …). |
| `actions` |  | `Array&lt;MessageBoxAction \| (string & {})> \| MessageBoxAction \| (string & {})` | One action or an array: MessageBox.Action values or custom texts. A single (non-array) action is emphasized. |
| `emphasizedAction` |  | `MessageBoxAction \| (string & {}) \| null` | The emphasized action; the shortcuts emphasize OK only when `actions` is not given. null for none. |
| `onClose` |  | `(action: MessageBoxAction \| (string & {}) \| null) => void` | Called with the pressed action, or null when the box was closed with Escape. |
| `id` |  | `string` | Id of the dialog element. |
| `initialFocus` |  | `MessageBoxAction \| (string & {}) \| null` | The action (or button text) whose button gets the initial focus. |
| `details` |  | `string \| object \| (() => Promise&lt;string \| object>)` | Shown after "View Details": a string, an object (as JSON) or a function returning a promise of one. |
| `contentWidth` |  | `string` | CSS width of the dialog content. |
| `verticalScrolling` |  | `boolean` | Scrolling of the content (UI5 default true for both). |
| `horizontalScrolling` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `closeOnNavigation` |  | `boolean` | Accepted and ignored: smart-kit has no router (ADR-010) and no dependent-control lifecycle. |
| `dependentOn` |  | `unknown` |  |
| `styleClass` |  | `string` | Accepted and ignored (ADR-032 rule 7). |
| `textDirection` |  | `string` |  |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessageItem`

`$MessageItemSettings` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$MessageItemSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `type` |  | `MessageType` | Type of the message. |
| `title` |  | `string` |  |
| `subtitle` |  | `string` |  |
| `description` |  | `string` |  |
| `counter` |  | `number` | A number shown at the end of the item (how often the message occurs). |
| `groupName` |  | `string` | The group of the item when the view has `groupItems`. |
| `activeTitle` |  | `boolean` | The title is a link (fires onActiveTitlePress). |
| `markupDescription` |  | `boolean` | UI5 renders the description as HTML; smart-kit renders it as plain text. |
| `longtextUrl` |  | `string` | A URL with a long text, loaded when the details page opens (shown as plain text; fires onLongtextLoaded). |
| `link` |  | `ReactNode` | A link shown on the details page (`link` aggregation). |
| `text` |  | `string` | Text of the item (inherited from sap.ui.core.Item); like UI5, the view shows `title`, not `text`. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` | Inherited from sap.ui.core.Item; like UI5, the view does not use it. |

### `default` — component

sap.m.MessageItem: declares one message of a MessageView / MessagePopover (renders nothing on its own).

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessageItem';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `type` |  | `MessageType` | Type of the message. |
| `title` |  | `string` |  |
| `subtitle` |  | `string` |  |
| `description` |  | `string` |  |
| `counter` |  | `number` | A number shown at the end of the item (how often the message occurs). |
| `groupName` |  | `string` | The group of the item when the view has `groupItems`. |
| `activeTitle` |  | `boolean` | The title is a link (fires onActiveTitlePress). |
| `markupDescription` |  | `boolean` | UI5 renders the description as HTML; smart-kit renders it as plain text. |
| `longtextUrl` |  | `string` | A URL with a long text, loaded when the details page opens (shown as plain text; fires onLongtextLoaded). |
| `link` |  | `ReactNode` | A link shown on the details page (`link` aggregation). |
| `text` |  | `string` | Text of the item (inherited from sap.ui.core.Item); like UI5, the view shows `title`, not `text`. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` | Inherited from sap.ui.core.Item; like UI5, the view does not use it. |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessagePopover`

`$MessagePopoverSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `MessagePopover$ActiveTitlePressEvent` (type) · `MessagePopover$AfterCloseEvent` (interface) · `MessagePopover$AfterOpenEvent` (interface) · `MessagePopover$BeforeCloseEvent` (interface) · `MessagePopover$BeforeOpenEvent` (interface) · `MessagePopover$ItemSelectEvent` (type) · `MessagePopover$ListSelectEvent` (type)

### `$MessagePopoverSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `placement` |  | `VerticalPlacementType` | Position of the popover: below, or above when there is no room ('Vertical'), always 'Top' or 'Bottom'. |
| `initiallyExpanded` |  | `boolean` | Opens with the message list shown; false opens with only the header and filter. |
| `groupItems` |  | `boolean` | Groups the items by `groupName`. |
| `onItemSelect` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$ItemSelectEvent) => void` |  |
| `onListSelect` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$ListSelectEvent) => void` |  |
| `onActiveTitlePress` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$ActiveTitlePressEvent) => void` |  |
| `onBeforeOpen` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$BeforeOpenEvent) => void` |  |
| `onAfterOpen` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$AfterOpenEvent) => void` |  |
| `onBeforeClose` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$BeforeCloseEvent) => void` |  |
| `onAfterClose` |  | `(e: MessagePopover$AfterCloseEvent) => void` |  |
| `aria-label` |  | `string` | Accessible name of the popover (default "Messages"). |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;MessagePopover>` |  |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | `MessageItem` elements. |
| `items` |  | `Message[]` | Extension: core messages (ADR-023), shown like MessageItems: text as title, target as subtitle and active title. |
| `asyncDescriptionHandler` |  | `(config: MessageViewAsyncDescriptionConfig) => void` | Loads the description of an item with a `longtextUrl` when its details open (the details page is busy meanwhile). Default: `defaultAsyncDescriptionHandler` (GET `longtextUrl`, as UI5's default handler). |
| `headerButton` |  | `ReactNode` | A button at the end of the list header (MessagePopover `headerButton`). |
| `onLongtextLoaded` |  | `() => void` | Fired after the description of an item with a `longtextUrl` has been loaded (`asyncDescriptionHandler`). |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessageStrip`

`$MessageStripSettings` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$MessageStripSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `text` |  | `string` |  |
| `type` |  | `MessageType` | Type of the message ('None' is shown as Information). |
| `showIcon` |  | `boolean` | Shows the icon of the type (or `customIcon`). |
| `colorSet` |  | `'Default' \| 'ColorSet1' \| 'ColorSet2'` | sap.m.MessageStripColorSet (1.143): 'Default' uses the colours of `type`; ColorSet1 / ColorSet2 use `colorScheme`. |
| `colorScheme` |  | `number` | 1–10, only with ColorSet1 / ColorSet2. |
| `customIcon` |  | `ReactNode` | Replaces the icon of the type (only with showIcon). UI5 takes an icon URI; smart-kit takes an element (ADR-032). |
| `showCloseButton` |  | `boolean` | Shows a close button. |
| `link` |  | `ReactNode` | A link after the text (`link` aggregation). |
| `controls` |  | `ReactNode[]` | Links referenced from a formatted text (`controls` aggregation); shown after the text. |
| `enableFormattedText` |  | `boolean` | UI5 parses `text` as limited HTML; smart-kit never parses HTML, so the text stays plain. Use `children` instead. |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | Extension: rich content instead of `text`. |
| `onClose` |  | `() => void` |  |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;MessageStrip>` |  |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/MessageToast`

`default` (const) · `MessageToastOptions` (interface)

### `default` — const

```ts
const default: { show(message: ReactNode, o?: MessageToastOptions): void; }
```

### `MessageToastOptions` — interface

sap.m.MessageToast `show` options (1.154).

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `duration` |  | `number` | Milliseconds before the close animation starts (UI5 default 3000). |
| `width` |  | `string` | CSS width (UI5 default 15em; the default comes from the token --sk-toast-width). |
| `my` |  | `Dock` | The point of the toast that is aligned (UI5 default 'CenterBottom'). |
| `at` |  | `Dock` | The point of the reference (`of`) the toast is aligned to (UI5 default 'CenterBottom'). |
| `of` |  | `Element` | The reference element (default: the window). |
| `offset` |  | `string` | Offset 'x y' in pixels (UI5 default '0 0'). |
| `collision` |  | `string` | 'fit', 'flip' or 'none', per axis (UI5 default 'fit fit'); smart-kit always keeps the toast inside the window. |
| `onClose` |  | `() => void` | Called after the toast has closed. |
| `autoClose` |  | `boolean` | Closes as soon as the user touches or clicks the screen (UI5 default true). |
| `animationTimingFunction` |  | `string` | CSS timing function of the close animation (UI5 default 'ease'). |
| `animationDuration` |  | `number` | Duration of the close animation in milliseconds (UI5 default 1000). |
| `closeOnBrowserNavigation` |  | `boolean` | Accepted and ignored: smart-kit has no router (ADR-010). |

### `MessageTypeFilter` — type

The type filter of the list: a message type, or 'All'.

```ts
export type MessageTypeFilter = MessageType | 'All';
```

### `MessageView$ActiveTitlePressEvent` — interface

Event object of `onActiveTitlePress` (sap.m.MessageView#activeTitlePress).

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `item` | **yes** | `MessageViewItem` |  |

### `MessageView$ItemSelectEvent` — interface

Event object of `onItemSelect` (sap.m.MessageView#itemSelect): the item whose details are shown.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `item` | **yes** | `MessageViewItem` |  |
| `messageTypeFilter` | **yes** | `MessageTypeFilter` |  |

### `MessageView$ListSelectEvent` — interface

Event object of `onListSelect` (sap.m.MessageView#listSelect): the list shown, filtered by type or not.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `messageTypeFilter` | **yes** | `MessageTypeFilter` |  |

### `MessageViewAsyncDescriptionConfig` — interface

Argument of `asyncDescriptionHandler` (sap.m.MessageView~asyncDescriptionHandler). The handler loads the description of `item` (a copy of the item), sets `item.description` (UI5: `item.setDescription`) and calls `promise.resolve()`; `promise.reject()` shows the details without it.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `item` | **yes** | `MessageViewItem` |  |
| `promise` | **yes** | `{ resolve(value?: unknown): void; reject(reason?: unknown): void }` |  |

### `MessageViewItem` — type

An item as passed to event handlers: its properties, its React key, and (extension) the core message it came from.

```ts
export type MessageViewItem = $MessageItemSettings & { key?: string; message?: Message };
```

### `ObjectIdentifier$TitlePressEvent` — interface

Event object of ObjectIdentifier's titlePress.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `domRef` | **yes** | `HTMLElement` | The title element. |

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/ObjectMarker';`

### `ObjectMarker$PressEvent` — interface

Event object of ObjectMarker's press.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `type` | **yes** | `ObjectMarkerType` | The marker type. |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/ObjectNumber`

`$ObjectNumberSettings` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$ObjectNumberSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `number` |  | `string \| number` | The number. In Default mode it is shown as given; Currency and Unit modes format it with the locale. |
| `emphasized` |  | `boolean` | Bold number. |
| `state` |  | `ValueState` | Colour. |
| `unit` |  | `string` | Unit after the number; the ISO currency code in Currency mode. |
| `textAlign` |  | `TextAlignValue` | sap.ui.core.TextAlign. |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | The number is clickable; fires onPress. |
| `reactiveAreaMode` |  | `ReactiveAreaMode` | Overlay enlarges the clickable area of an active number. |
| `inverted` |  | `boolean` | Filled background in the state colour. |
| `displayMode` |  | `ObjectNumberDisplayMode` | Default shows the number as given; Currency formats it like Currency with `unit` as the currency; Unit formats it as a decimal. |
| `maxPrecision` |  | `number` | Fraction digits in Currency and Unit modes (Currency: padded like Currency; default the currency's digits). |
| `useSymbol` |  | `boolean` | Currency mode: shows the currency symbol instead of the code. |
| `emptyIndicatorMode` |  | `EmptyIndicatorMode` | Shows "–" when number is empty. |
| `onPress` |  | `(e: Record&lt;string, never>) => void` | Fired when an active number is pressed. |
| `defaultChecked` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |
| `defaultValue` |  | `string \| number \| readonly string[] \| undefined` |  |

### `default` — component

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/ObjectNumber';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `number` |  | `string \| number` | The number. In Default mode it is shown as given; Currency and Unit modes format it with the locale. |
| `emphasized` |  | `boolean` | Bold number. |
| `state` |  | `ValueState` | Colour. |
| `unit` |  | `string` | Unit after the number; the ISO currency code in Currency mode. |
| `textAlign` |  | `TextAlignValue` | sap.ui.core.TextAlign. |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | The number is clickable; fires onPress. |
| `reactiveAreaMode` |  | `ReactiveAreaMode` | Overlay enlarges the clickable area of an active number. |
| `inverted` |  | `boolean` | Filled background in the state colour. |
| `displayMode` |  | `ObjectNumberDisplayMode` | Default shows the number as given; Currency formats it like Currency with `unit` as the currency; Unit formats it as a decimal. |
| `maxPrecision` |  | `number` | Fraction digits in Currency and Unit modes (Currency: padded like Currency; default the currency's digits). |
| `useSymbol` |  | `boolean` | Currency mode: shows the currency symbol instead of the code. |
| `emptyIndicatorMode` |  | `EmptyIndicatorMode` | Shows "–" when number is empty. |
| `onPress` |  | `(e: Record&lt;string, never>) => void` | Fired when an active number is pressed. |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/ObjectStatus`

`$ObjectStatusSettings` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$ObjectStatusSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` |  | `string` | Label shown before the text as "title:". |
| `text` |  | `string` | Status text. |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | Text and icon are clickable; fires onPress. |
| `reactiveAreaMode` |  | `ReactiveAreaMode` | Overlay enlarges the clickable area of an active status. |
| `state` |  | `ValueState \| IndicationColor` | Colour: a ValueState or an IndicationColor. |
| `inverted` |  | `boolean` | Filled background in the state colour. |
| `icon` |  | `ReactNode` | Icon before the text (a React node, e.g. a lucide icon). |
| `emptyIndicatorMode` |  | `EmptyIndicatorMode` | Shows "–" when text is empty. |
| `stateAnnouncementText` |  | `string` | Text read by screen readers for the state, instead of the default ("Error", "Warning", …). |
| `onPress` |  | `(e: Record&lt;string, never>) => void` | Fired when an active status is pressed. |
| `defaultChecked` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |
| `defaultValue` |  | `string \| number \| readonly string[] \| undefined` |  |
| `suppressContentEditableWarning` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |
| `suppressHydrationWarning` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |

### `default` — component

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/ObjectStatus';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` |  | `string` | Label shown before the text as "title:". |
| `text` |  | `string` | Status text. |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | Text and icon are clickable; fires onPress. |
| `reactiveAreaMode` |  | `ReactiveAreaMode` | Overlay enlarges the clickable area of an active status. |
| `state` |  | `ValueState \| IndicationColor` | Colour: a ValueState or an IndicationColor. |
| `inverted` |  | `boolean` | Filled background in the state colour. |
| `icon` |  | `ReactNode` | Icon before the text (a React node, e.g. a lucide icon). |
| `emptyIndicatorMode` |  | `EmptyIndicatorMode` | Shows "–" when text is empty. |
| `stateAnnouncementText` |  | `string` | Text read by screen readers for the state, instead of the default ("Error", "Warning", …). |
| `onPress` |  | `(e: Record&lt;string, never>) => void` | Fired when an active status is pressed. |
| `defaultChecked` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |
| `defaultValue` |  | `string \| number \| readonly string[] \| undefined` |  |
| `suppressContentEditableWarning` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |
| `suppressHydrationWarning` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |
| `accessKey` |  | `string \| undefined` |  |
| `autoCapitalize` |  | `"off" \| "none" \| "on" \| "sentences" \| "words" \| "characters" \| undefined \| (string & {})` |  |
| `autoFocus` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |
| `className` |  | `string \| undefined` |  |
| `contentEditable` |  | `Booleanish \| "inherit" \| "plaintext-only" \| undefined` |  |
| `contextMenu` |  | `string \| undefined` |  |
| `dir` |  | `string \| undefined` |  |
| `draggable` |  | `Booleanish \| undefined` |  |

| `hidden` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |

| `lang` |  | `string \| undefined` |  |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/OverflowToolbar`

`$OverflowToolbarSettings` (interface) · `default` (component) · `OverflowToolbar$PressEvent` (interface) · `OverflowToolbarLayoutData` (interface)

### `$OverflowToolbarSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `width` |  | `string` | Width (CSS length; default 100%). |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | The whole toolbar is clickable and fires onPress. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` | Enabled; a disabled toolbar disables its content. |
| `height` |  | `string` | Height (CSS length; default: the control height). |
| `design` |  | `ToolbarDesign` | Background design (Auto: transparent). |
| `style` |  | `ToolbarStyle` | Clear removes the border. |
| `ariaHasPopup` |  | `AriaHasPopup` | aria-haspopup of an active toolbar. |
| `asyncMode` |  | `boolean` | Accepted for compatibility; the layout is always computed synchronously. |
| `onPress` |  | `(e: OverflowToolbar$PressEvent) => void` | Fired when an active toolbar is pressed outside its interactive content; `srcControl` is the clicked element. |
| `children` |  | `ReactNode` | Toolbar content: buttons, texts, ToolbarSpacer; `layoutData` on a child sets its overflow priority. |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;OverflowToolbar>` |  |
| `defaultChecked` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |
| `defaultValue` |  | `string \| number \| readonly string[] \| undefined` |  |
| `suppressContentEditableWarning` |  | `boolean \| undefined` |  |

### `default` — component

Instance methods of OverflowToolbar.

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/m/OverflowToolbar';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `width` |  | `string` | Width (CSS length; default 100%). |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | The whole toolbar is clickable and fires onPress. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` | Enabled; a disabled toolbar disables its content. |
| `height` |  | `string` | Height (CSS length; default: the control height). |

### `$ColumnSettings` — interface

A column (sap.m.Column) of the `columns` aggregation.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | **yes** | `string` | Column id (the element id in UI5); used in events and for `groupBy`. |
| `header` | **yes** | `ReactNode` | Column header (sap.m.Column aggregation `header`). |
| `cell` | **yes** | `(row: Row) => ReactNode` | Cell of a row: the column list item template (React form of the items binding). |
| `importance` |  | `Priority` | Importance for the automatic pop-in (`autoPopinMode`); "None" counts as "Medium". |
| `hAlign` |  | `'Begin' \| 'End' \| 'Center' \| 'Left' \| 'Right' \| 'Initial'` | Horizontal alignment of the header and the cells. |
| `width` |  | `string` | Column width (CSS length). |
| `visible` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `demandPopin` |  | `boolean` | Without `autoPopinMode`: below `minScreenWidth` the column goes into the pop-in instead of being hidden. |
| `minScreenWidth` |  | `string` | Without `autoPopinMode`: a sap.m.ScreenSize ("Phone", "Tablet", "Desktop", …) or a CSS width below which the column is hidden or popped in. |
| `popinDisplay` |  | `PopinDisplay` | How the pop-in shows header and value. |
| `footer` |  | `ReactNode` | Footer of the column (sap.m.Column aggregation `footer`). |
| `groupValue` |  | `(row: Row) => string` | Text of the grouping header (value of the group property; smart-kit addition). |

### `$ListItemActionSettings` — interface

An action of a row (sap.m.ListItemAction of the list item aggregation `actions`). "Edit" and "Delete" bring their own icon and text (Ctrl+E and Delete on the focused row press them); "Navigation" is not shown but makes the row navigable (chevron and `itemPress`).

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `key` |  | `string` | Key of the action (React key; tells actions apart in `itemActionPress`). Defaults to the type and the text. |
| `type` |  | `ListItemActionType` |  |
| `text` |  | `string` | Text (tooltip of the button, text of the overflow menu item) of a "Custom" action. |
| `icon` |  | `ReactNode` | Icon of a "Custom" action. |
| `visible` |  | `boolean` |  |

### `$TableSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `columns` | **yes** | `$ColumnSettings[]` | Columns (sap.m.Column). |
| `items` | **yes** | `Row[]` | The rows (React form of the items binding: data, rendered with the columns' `cell`). |
| `keyOf` | **yes** | `(row: Row) => string` | Key of a row (React key, selection and events). |
| `aria-label` |  | `string` | Accessible name of the table when there is no `headerText`. |
| `headerText` |  | `string` | Title shown above the table. |
| `headerLevel` |  | `TitleLevel` | Heading level of `headerText` ("Auto" = H2). |
| `footerText` |  | `string` | Text shown below the table. |
| `headerToolbar` |  | `ReactNode` | Toolbar above the column headers. |
| `infoToolbar` |  | `ReactNode` | Info bar below the header toolbar. |
| `mode` |  | `ListMode` | Selection mode: "MultiSelect" check boxes, "SingleSelectLeft" radio buttons, "SingleSelectMaster" the row, "Delete" a delete button per row. |
| `selectedKeys` |  | `string[]` | Selected row keys (controlled; smart-kit addition). Without it the table keeps the selection itself. |
| `includeItemInSelection` |  | `boolean` | A click on the row toggles its selection (selection modes). |
| `rememberSelections` |  | `boolean` | Keeps the selection when the items change (uncontrolled selection). |
| `multiSelectMode` |  | `MultiSelectMode` | "ClearAll" shows a "clear selection" button instead of the "select all" check box. |
| `width` |  | `string` |  |
| `fixedLayout` |  | `boolean \| 'Strict'` | `table-layout: fixed`; false: column widths follow the content. |
| `showOverlay` |  | `boolean` | Greys out the table (e.g. filters changed but not applied). |
| `backgroundDesign` |  | `'Solid' \| 'Translucent' \| 'Transparent'` | Background: "Solid", "Translucent" (the page shows through the theme's table colors) or "Transparent". |
| `alternateRowColors` |  | `boolean` | Every other row with a background. |
| `showSeparators` |  | `ListSeparators` | Row separators: "All", "Inner" (not after the last row) or "None". |
| `inset` |  | `boolean` | Space around the table. |
| `popinLayout` |  | `PopinLayout` | Arrangement of the pop-in: "Block" (one below the other) or "GridSmall" / "GridLarge" (a grid of small or large cells). |
| `noDataText` |  | `string` | Text when there are no items; default "No items available.". |
| `noData` |  | `ReactNode` | Content when there are no items (replaces `noDataText`). |
| `showNoData` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `busy` |  | `boolean` | Shows the busy indicator (rows are loading). |
| `enableBusyIndicator` |  | `boolean` |  |
| `growing` |  | `boolean` | Shows the items in pages: a "More" button or scrolling loads the next one. |
| `growingThreshold` |  | `number` | Items per page when the table grows over `items` itself (no `hasMore`). |
| `growingTriggerText` |  | `string` | Text of the "More" button (default "More"). |
| `growingScrollToLoad` |  | `boolean` | Load the next page when the end of the table scrolls into view instead of a button. |
| `growingDirection` |  | `ListGrowingDirection` | "Upwards": the "More" button (or scroll to load) is at the top and the items are shown in reverse order, the first one at the bottom (chat-like lists). |
| `hasMore` |  | `boolean` | More items can be loaded by the parent (`onUpdateStarted` with reason "Growing" asks for them; smart-kit addition). |
| `keyboardMode` |  | `ListKeyboardMode` | "Navigation": arrow keys move between the cells; "Edit": Tab moves between the input fields of the cells, arrow up / down moves to the same field of the previous / next row. |
| `showUnread` |  | `boolean` | Shows the unread indicator (bold rows) of the rows that `rowUnread` marks. |
| `rowUnread` |  | `(row: Row) => boolean` | Whether a row is unread (sap.m.ListItemBase `unread` of the row; shown with `showUnread`). |
| `modeAnimationOn` |  | `boolean` | The selection controls animate in when `mode` changes. |
| `itemActionCount` |  | `number` | Maximum number of row actions (`rowActions`) shown in a column at the end; more go into an overflow menu. 0–2 enable the actions (mode "Delete" then has no effect); -1 does not show them. |
| `rowActions` |  | `(row: Row) => $ListItemActionSettings[]` | Actions of a row (sap.m.ListItemBase aggregation `actions` of the row). |
| `swipeContent` |  | `ReactNode` | Content shown at the end of a row after a touch swipe (e.g. a button); hidden again by a tap elsewhere in the table or `swipeOut`. |
| `swipeDirection` |  | `SwipeDirection` | Swipe direction that shows `swipeContent`. |
| `contextMenu` |  | `ReactNode \| ((listItem: Row) => ReactNode)` | Context menu of the rows (opened by right-click or Shift+F10 / the menu key on a row); a function gets the row (the binding context UI5 sets on the menu). |
| `autoPopinMode` |  | `boolean` | Moves columns into the pop-in by `importance` as the width shrinks. |
| `hiddenInPopin` |  | `Priority[]` | Importances whose popped-in columns are not shown in the pop-in area ("Show Details" off). |
| `contextualWidth` |  | `string` | Width used for the pop-in: "Inherit" (the table's), "Desktop", "Tablet", "Phone" or a CSS width. |
| `sticky` |  | `Sticky[]` | Parts that stay visible when the page scrolls; "ColumnHeaders" keeps the column headers. |
| `navigable` |  | `boolean` | Rows can navigate: a chevron and `onItemPress` on click and Enter (type "Navigation" of the items; smart-kit addition). |
| `groupBy` |  | `string` | Column id to group by (React form of a grouping sorter; smart-kit addition). |
| `groupSummary` |  | `(row: Row) => GroupSummary \| undefined` | Subtotals of the group starting with `row`: row count and cells by column id (docs/23). |
| `totals` |  | `{ label: ReactNode; cells: Record&lt;string, ReactNode> }` | Totals row after the last row: cells by column id (docs/23). |
| `tree` |  | `{ nodeOf(row: Row): HierarchyNode \| undefined; toggle(row: Row): void }` | Tree rows (docs/26 §3.10): indentation and toggle in the first column. |
| `inlineActions` |  | `(row: Row) => ReactNode` | Actions in a column at the end of each row. |
| `changedKeys` |  | `Set&lt;string>` | Keys of rows marked as changed (continuous working mode). |
| `rowHighlight` |  | `(row: Row) => string \| undefined` | Highlight of a row (sap.m.ListItemBase highlight: "error", "warning", "success", "information"; smart-kit internal). |
| `rowMarker` |  | `(row: Row) => ReactNode` | Content after the first cell (e.g. draft markers). |
| `onSelectionChange` |  | `(e: Table$SelectionChangeEvent) => void` |  |
| `onItemPress` |  | `(e: Table$ItemPressEvent) => void` |  |
| `onDelete` |  | `(e: Table$DeleteEvent) => void` | A row's delete button was pressed (mode "Delete"). |
| `onUpdateStarted` |  | `(e: Table$UpdateStartedEvent) => void` | More items are needed (reason "Growing"). |
| `onUpdateFinished` |  | `(e: Table$UpdateFinishedEvent) => void` | The items changed. |
| `onPopinChanged` |  | `(e: Table$PopinChangedEvent) => void` | The set of columns in the pop-in changed. |
| `onPaste` |  | `(e: Table$PasteEvent) => void` | Text was pasted into the table (e.g. from a spreadsheet). |
| `onBeforeOpenContextMenu` |  | `(e: Table$BeforeOpenContextMenuEvent) => void` | The context menu of a row is about to open (`preventDefault` keeps it closed). |
| `onSwipe` |  | `(e: Table$SwipeEvent) => void` | A row was swiped, before `swipeContent` is shown (`preventDefault` cancels). |
| `onItemActionPress` |  | `(e: Table$ItemActionPressEvent) => void` | A row action (`rowActions`) was pressed. |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;Table>` | Instance methods (sap.m.Table / sap.m.ListBase). |

### `PopinDisplay` — type

sap.m.PopinDisplay

```ts
export type PopinDisplay = 'Block' | 'Inline' | 'WithoutHeader';
```

### `Table$BeforeOpenContextMenuEvent` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `listItem` | **yes** | `Row` | The row in which the context menu was opened. |
| `column` | **yes** | `$ColumnSettings \| undefined` | The column in which the context menu was opened (undefined outside the columns, e.g. the selection cell). |
| `preventDefault` | **yes** | `() => void` | Keeps the context menu closed (the browser's own menu opens). |

### `Table$DeleteEvent` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `listItem` | **yes** | `Row` |  |

### `Table$ItemActionPressEvent` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `action` | **yes** | `$ListItemActionSettings` | The pressed action. |
| `listItem` | **yes** | `Row` | The row of the action. |

### `Table$ItemPressEvent` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `listItem` | **yes** | `Row` |  |
| `srcControl` | **yes** | `HTMLElement` | The element that was clicked. |

### `Table$PasteEvent` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data` | **yes** | `string[][]` | Pasted text as rows of cells (tab-separated columns, one line per row). |
| `preventDefault` | **yes** | `() => void` | Stops the browser's default paste handling. |

### `Table$PopinChangedEvent` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `hasPopin` | **yes** | `boolean` |  |
| `visibleInPopin` | **yes** | `$ColumnSettings[]` | Columns shown in the pop-in area. |
| `hiddenInPopin` | **yes** | `$ColumnSettings[]` | Columns moved out of the table whose pop-in is hidden (`hiddenInPopin`). |

### `Table$SelectionChangeEvent` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `listItem` | **yes** | `Row \| undefined` | The row whose selection changed (the first one when several changed). |
| `listItems` | **yes** | `Row[]` | The rows whose selection changed. |
| `selected` | **yes** | `boolean` | Whether they were selected (true) or deselected (false). |
| `selectAll` | **yes** | `boolean` | The change came from the "select all" checkbox. |

### `Table$SwipeEvent` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `listItem` | **yes** | `Row` | The swiped row. |
| `swipeContent` | **yes** | `ReactNode` | The `swipeContent` shown at the end of the row. |
| `srcControl` | **yes** | `HTMLElement` | The element the swipe started on. |
| `swipeDirection` | **yes** | `'BeginToEnd' \| 'EndToBegin'` | "BeginToEnd" or "EndToBegin" (left to right / right to left in left-to-right languages). |
| `preventDefault` | **yes** | `() => void` | Cancels the swipe (the swipe content is not shown). |

### `Table$UpdateFinishedEvent` — type

```ts
export type Table$UpdateFinishedEvent = Table$UpdateStartedEvent;
```

### `Table$UpdateStartedEvent` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `reason` | **yes** | `string` | "Growing" when more items are requested, "Change" otherwise. |
| `actual` | **yes** | `number` | Number of items shown. |
| `total` | **yes** | `number \| undefined` | Total number of items, when known. |

| `className` |  | `string` |  |

| Member | Required | Type | Description |

| `enableReorder` |  | `boolean` | Whether the group criteria can be reordered (move buttons). |
| `queryLimit` |  | `number` | The maximum number of group criteria; -1: no limit. |
| `messageStrip` |  | `ReactNode` | A message strip shown above the rows (BasePanel `messageStrip`, a sap.m.MessageStrip element). |
| `p13nData` |  | `GroupItem[]` | The personalization state shown (BasePanel.setP13nData): the grouped items first, in group order. A new array starts the panel again. |
| `onChange` |  | `(e: GroupPanel$ChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the user changes the state (BasePanel `change`; reason "showifgrouped" for the check box). |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;GroupPanel>` |  |

| `onChange` |  | `(e: SelectionPanel$ChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the user changes the state (BasePanel `change`). |

### `UploadItemInstance` — type

An UploadItem in event objects and ref methods, with the UploadItem methods.

```ts
export type UploadItemInstance = UploadItem & { id: string; /** Downloads the file (`askForLocation`: a save dialog where the browser has one). */ download(askForLocation?: boolean): void; /** The item fails `fileTypes`, `mediaTypes`, `maxFileNameLength` or `maxFileSize`. */ isRestricted(): boolean; /** Aborts a running upload. */ terminateUpload(): void; };
```

### `$BulletMicroChartSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/ui/integration/cards/AnalyticalContent`

`$AnalyticalContentSettings` (interface) · `CardAnnotationSettings` (interface) · `CardDataProps` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$AnalyticalContentSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `entitySet` | **yes** | `string` | Entity set name as in `$metadata`, e.g. "Orders". |
| `service` |  | `string` | Name of the service in `SmartKitProvider` `services`; default: the provider's `defaultService`. |
| `chartAnnotationPath` |  | `string` | UI.Chart, e.g. "com.sap.vocabularies.UI.v1.Chart#ByStatus"; default: from the presentation variant, else the unqualified chart. |
| `presentationAnnotationPath` |  | `string` | Qualified `UI.PresentationVariant` term, e.g. "@UI.PresentationVariant#Top5" (sort, MaxItems, chart). |
| `selectionAnnotationPath` |  | `string` | Qualified `UI.SelectionVariant` term, e.g. "@UI.SelectionVariant#Open" (fixed filters). |
| `filters` |  | `FilterState` | Filter state (from `useFilterState` / `SmartFilterBar`), applied to the request. |
| `maxItems` |  | `number` | Maximum number of data points (default 4 for column, bar and donut; OVP fixed card layout). |
| `navigation` |  | `'dataPointNav' \| 'headerNav' \| 'noHeaderNav'` | OVP `navigation`: "dataPointNav" (default: header and data points), "headerNav" (header only), "noHeaderNav" (data points only). |
| `onDataPointPress` |  | `(dimension: string, value: string) => void` | Data point press: the dimension value of the selected point. |
| `height` |  | `number` | Height of the chart area (CSS length; default the card token). |
| `renderChart` |  | `(chart: ChartModel, data: ChartDatum[]) => ReactNode` | Replaces the drawing (e.g. with a chart library), for chart types SimpleChart does not draw. |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/ui/integration/cards/CalendarContent`

`$CalendarContentSettings` (interface) · `CalendarLegendItem` (interface) · `CalendarSpecialDate` (interface) · `CalendarWeekNumbering` (type) · `CardAnnotationSettings` (interface) · `CardDataProps` (interface) · `default` (component) · `EventItemPaths` (interface)

### `$CalendarContentSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `entitySet` | **yes** | `string` | Entity set name as in `$metadata`, e.g. "Orders". |
| `service` |  | `string` | Name of the service in `SmartKitProvider` `services`; default: the provider's `defaultService`. |
| `item` |  | `EventItemPaths` | Property paths of the appointments; default Communication.Event. |

| `filters` |  | `FilterState` | Filter state (from `useFilterState` / `SmartFilterBar`), applied to the request. |
| `date` |  | `string` | Initially selected day, "YYYY-MM-DD" (default today). |
| `maxItems` |  | `number` | Visible appointments of the selected day (default 2). |
| `maxLegendItems` |  | `number` | Visible legend items (default 2); the rest are counted in "More (x)". |
| `noItemsText` |  | `string` | Text when there are no items. |
| `specialDates` |  | `CalendarSpecialDate[]` |  |
| `legendItems` |  | `CalendarLegendItem[]` | Legend items (texts of special day types, for example); the appointment types found are added (one circle each). |
| `onAppointmentPress` |  | `(row: Row) => void` |  |
| `onMorePress` |  | `(date: string) => void` | Press of "More"; default: the list page of the entity set. |
| `onSelect` |  | `(date: string) => void` | Called when the user selects a day. |
| `calendarWeekNumbering` |  | `CalendarWeekNumbering` | First day of the week and week numbering (manifest `calendarWeekNumbering`, sap.ui.core.date.CalendarWeekNumbering): "Default" (from the locale), "ISO_8601" (Monday, ISO weeks), "MiddleEastern" (Saturday), "WesternTraditional" (Sunday). |
| `use12HourFormat` |  | `boolean` | Times of the appointments in the 12-hour format (manifest `use12HourFormat`); default the 24-hour format. |
| `now` |  | `Date` | Reference time for "today" (default now). |

| `type` | **yes** | `string` |  |

| `type` | **yes** | `string` | Legend type (matches `CalendarLegendItem.type`). |

| `use12HourFormat` |  | `boolean` | Times of the appointments in the 12-hour format (manifest `use12HourFormat`); default the 24-hour format. |
| `now` |  | `Date` | Reference time for "today" (default now). |

| Member | Required | Type | Description |

| `showStateIcon` |  | `boolean` | Icon in front of the info value when it has a state (integration card `info.showStateIcon`). |

| `headerNavigation` |  | `boolean` | Header press default; false: the header does not navigate. |
| `annotationPath` |  | `string` | UI.LineItem (or another collection) of the content, e.g. "com.sap.vocabularies.UI.v1.LineItem#Card". |

### `default` — component

Instance methods of Currency.

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/ui/unified/Currency';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` |  | `number \| null` | The amount. `null` counts as empty (see emptyIndicatorMode). |
| `stringValue` |  | `string` | The exact amount as a decimal string (Edm.Decimal); used instead of `value`, avoiding float rounding. |
| `currency` |  | `string` | ISO 4217 currency code. |
| `maxPrecision` |  | `number` | Fraction digits the amount is padded to, so amounts in different currencies align (default: the currency's digits). |
| `useSymbol` |  | `boolean` | Shows the currency symbol (€, $) instead of the code where one exists. |
| `emptyIndicatorMode` |  | `EmptyIndicatorMode` | Shows "–" when there is no amount. |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/uxap/AnchorBar`

`$AnchorBarSettings` (interface) · `AnchorBar$PressEvent` (interface) · `AnchorItem` (interface) · `default` (component)

### `$AnchorBarSettings` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `content` |  | `AnchorItem[]` | The anchors: one per section, with the subsections for the popover. |
| `selectedButton` |  | `string` | Id of the selected anchor (section). |
| `showPopover` |  | `boolean` | Anchors of sections with several subsections open a menu of the subsections. |
| `upperCase` |  | `boolean` | Anchor texts in upper case. |
| `backgroundDesign` |  | `BackgroundDesign` | Background of the bar. |
| `width` |  | `string` | Width (CSS length; default 100%). |
| `active` |  | `boolean` | The whole bar is clickable and fires onPress. |
| `enabled` |  | `boolean` | Enabled; a disabled bar disables its anchors. |
| `height` |  | `string` | Height (CSS length; default: the bar height). |
| `design` |  | `ToolbarDesign` | Background design of the toolbar. |
| `style` |  | `ToolbarStyle` | Clear removes the border. |
| `ariaHasPopup` |  | `AriaHasPopup` | aria-haspopup of an active bar. |
| `onPress` |  | `(e: AnchorBar$PressEvent) => void` | Fired when an active bar is pressed outside its anchors; `srcControl` is the clicked element. |
| `onSelect` |  | `(sectionId: string, subSectionId?: string) => void` | Extension: an anchor (and optionally one of its subsections) was chosen; the page scrolls to it. |
| `ref` |  | `Ref&lt;AnchorBar>` |  |

### `AnchorBar$PressEvent` — interface

Event object of AnchorBar's press.

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `srcControl` | **yes** | `HTMLElement` | The clicked element. |

### `AnchorItem` — interface

| Member | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | **yes** | `string` |  |
| `title` | **yes** | `string` |  |
| `subSections` | **yes** | `{ id: string; title: string }[]` |  |
| `count` |  | `number` |  |

## `@rhfan2019/smart-kit/uxap/ObjectPageDynamicHeaderTitle`

`$ObjectPageDynamicHeaderTitleSettings` (type) · `default` (component) · `ObjectPageDynamicHeaderTitle$StateChangeEvent` (interface)

### `$ObjectPageDynamicHeaderTitleSettings` — type

sap.uxap.ObjectPageDynamicHeaderTitle: the same title as DynamicPageTitle.

```ts
export type $ObjectPageDynamicHeaderTitleSettings = $DynamicPageTitleSettings;
```

### `default` — component

sap.uxap.ObjectPageDynamicHeaderTitle (rendered by the ObjectPageLayout it is given to as `headerTitle`).

`import { default } from '@rhfan2019/smart-kit/uxap/ObjectPageDynamicHeaderTitle';`

| Prop | Required | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `heading` |  | `ReactNode` | The title (an h1 for a string; any element, e.g. a variant management, otherwise). |
| `snappedHeading` |  | `ReactNode` | Replaces `heading` while the header is snapped (e.g. a small avatar and the title). |
| `expandedHeading` |  | `ReactNode` | Replaces `heading` while the header is expanded. |
| `actions` |  | `ReactNode` | Actions on the right; they move into an overflow from right to left when space runs out (an OverflowToolbar). |
| `navigationActions` |  | `ReactNode` | Layout actions at the very right (DP Header Title (6)), e.g. FlexibleColumnLayoutActions. |
| `content` |  | `ReactNode` | Content in the middle of the title bar, e.g. key information (DP Header Title (4)). |
| `snappedContent` |  | `ReactNode` | Shown under the heading while the header is snapped (e.g. the filter information). |
| `expandedContent` |  | `ReactNode` | Shown under the heading while the header is expanded (e.g. the description). |
| `snappedTitleOnMobile` |  | `ReactNode` | On phones, a snapped title shows only this title and a toggle (DP Summary Line). |
| `breadcrumbs` |  | `ReactNode` | Breadcrumb links above the heading; inside a FlexibleColumnLayout only in full screen with one column. |
| `areaShrinkRatio` |  | `DynamicPageTitleShrinkRatio` | Flex-shrink factors of the heading, content and actions areas. |
| `backgroundDesign` |  | `BackgroundDesign` | Background of the title. |
| `onStateChange` |  | `(e: DynamicPageTitle$StateChangeEvent) => void` | Fired when the header is expanded or collapsed (by the user or by scrolling). |
